Let's formulate iterative algorithm:
Given positive a, sum=1. Define score=2n max c.
We want reduce. Choose temperature T = c_scale / beta, where c_scale=e.g. mean(c) or M. Weights w = softmax(c/T) = exp(c/T - max(c/T))/sum. Then compute G_i = ∂Σ w c /∂log a_i = a_i * H_i, where H_i =2∑_k w_k a_{k-i}. Instead of full weighted grad with w sum 1, if w normalized, G scale arbitrary. We can compute G = H * a. Center to preserve sum: Gc = G - np.average(G, weights=a) maybe. Because update δu = -η Gc, then d logS =∑a δu=0 if weight average with a. So subtract weighted mean μ = (a@G)/(a@a). Use a@a maybe. Then a_new = a * exp(clip(-η Gc_s, -cmax, cmax)). Need choose η. We can normalize Gc by its std, set η ~ step. If Gc has positive/negative; update where combined weighted convolution high (in terms of aH) down, low up.
Line search: try a few η (e.g. base, half, double) accept best score. Since score scale invariant, after update normalize.
Temperature anneal: start beta=5 (low focus, nearly all c weighted equally? weights ~uniform -> G ≈ a* (a*a total?) maybe negative? Let's see uniform weights w_k=1/(2n-1). H_j=2/(2n-1)∑_valid a_{k-j} ≈2/(2n) average over neighbors = ~S/(2n)≈1/(2n) constant. G_i≈a_i/(2n), weighted mean constant? Gc≈a_i*const - weighted mean, positive for large a? This reduces variance? It may push to equalize? Maybe okay. Increase beta to 50-200 for local.

But if weights uniform, H_j roughly local sum of a, not constant; gradient of L1 of coefficients. Could be used for global balanced.

Full polish algorithm more detailed:
Inputs: a array normalized, budget, seed, initial_step.
Variables:
- best=a.copy(), best_score=score(a)
- current=a, score
- momentum m=zeros
- step = 0.2? Since d scaled to std 1, step in log. Could start 0.1.
- beta = 20 (focus)
- noise=0.02
- stagnant counter
Loop while time<deadline:
  1. if time near, break.
  2. Ensure current positive: floor = 1e-12; current=np.maximum(current,floor); normalize.
  3. Compute c, M. Update best if score<best.
  4. If M <=0 or not finite reset.
  5. Weights w = softmax(beta*(c/M - 1.0)). For stability, if M=0 skip. Clamp exponents min -700.
  6. W = weighted_conv(a,w): use function `weighted_convolution(a, w)` returning L=2n-1 length weights w (sum1), compute V=∑_k w_k a_{k-j}. Implementation:
     - Nfft power of two >= 4n? For conv a length n and w_rev length 2n-1, total length n+2n-1-1=3n-1. N=nextpow2(3n) (or 4n). A=rfft(a,N); Wr=rfft(w_rev,N); Vfull=irfft(A*Wr,N). Need slice indices? We derived H=2(slice)[L-1:L-1+n] for H_j=2∑w a_{k-j}. Let L=2n-1. conv_len = n + (2n-1)-1 =3n-2? Actually if lengths n and 2n-1, full length = n+2n-1-1=3n-2. Index p = L-1+j = 2n-2 + j, j=0..n-1 => 2n-2..3n-3. The full output length 3n-2 last index 3n-3. Good. Slice [2n-2:3n-2]. Check length n. For n=2, L=3, conv a [a0,a1], w_rev [w2,w1,w0]. conv indices p=2: full[2]=a0*w2 + a1*w1? H0/2 =∑_k w_k a_{k} =w0 a0 +w2? Wait let's test n=2, j=0: ∑_k w_k a_{k}: k=0 a0, k=1 a1, k=2 out? So H0/2=w0 a0 + w1 a1. Our formula with w_rev: conv index L-1=2: full[2]=∑_i a_i w_rev[2-i] = i=0 a0 w0? w_rev[2]=w0; i=1 a1 w_rev[1]=w1. yes correct. good.
     - Use `np.real`.
  7. G = a * W. (W ~1/n, G ~1/n²? Wait W =∑ w a ~1/n. G ~1/n². Uniform W=1/n, G=1/n². scale tiny. We'll scale.)
  8. Project tangent: μ = (a @ G) / (a @ a). d = G - μ * a.
     Since G ~1/n², d small. scale = sqrt(a@d / a) maybe ~1/n². Normalize d by scale to order1. If scale <1e-14 stagnate.
  9. Momentum: m = 0.5*m +0.5*(d/scale). If |m| large clip.
  10. Noise: if noise>0, add rng.normal(0,noise,n) to m, maybe smooth noise in time? Smooth noise better to break local while not killing uniform? Use low-freq smooth. Generate noise = rng.normal(0, noise_smooth, n); optional apply FFT lowpass? Simpler. Add to m before step. Also random asymmetry.
  11. Choose step_size. For log update, `delta = -step * m`. Clip delta to [-2,2]. Candidate = current * exp(delta); floor; normalize.
  12. Line search: maybe try delta, -delta? Since gradient direction can be wrong near nonconvex. But if we use descent, -delta. Try factors [1,0.5,2] and maybe small opposite (0.1) to escape? Evaluate scores. Accept best candidate if score < current + accept_rel*current or if score < best.
     - If accepted, maybe increase step if improvement; decrease if not.
  13. If no accept, reduce step *=0.7, momentum*=0.5, stagnant++.
  14. If stagnant > threshold, restart: current=smooth_perturb(best), step=0.1, beta random 10-100, noise=0.05, momentum=0.
  15. Anneal beta: beta = beta_start + growth*time; or if improvement reduce? To flatten, use beta schedule from 5 to 100. We can maintain current_beta = 10 + 50*progress. Or random cycles.
  16. Optional: apply LP best-response every certain iterations or when stagnant.

def smooth_random_basis(n, rng, alpha=1.0):
  x=np.linspace(-1,1,n)
  z=np.zeros(n)
  D=20
  for m in range(1,D+1):
     amp_odd = alpha/(m**0.7) * (if odd? bias)
     amp_even = alpha/(2*m**0.7)
     z += amp_odd*rng.normal(0,1)*np.sin(np.pi*m*x)
     z += amp_even*rng.normal(0,1)*np.cos(np.pi*m*x)
  # add linear odd: z += alpha*rng.normal(0,1)*x
  a=np.exp(z); normalize sum1
```

def basis_search(n, budget, seed, dim=16, beta=20, start_theta=None):
  B=make_trig_basis(n,dim)
  if start_theta is None:
     th=rng.normal(0,0.5,dim)
     th[0]=1.0  # odd fundamental
  else: ...
  a=sequence_from_theta(B,th)
  best=a; best_score=score
  lr=0.1; beta_cur=beta
  for time:
    c,M; update
    w=softmax(beta_cur*(c/M-1))
    W=weighted_conv(a,w)
    G=a*W
    mu=(a@G)/(a@a)
    d=G - mu*a
    scale=np.sqrt(np.average(d*d, weights=a)) or 1
    if scale small: lr*=0.8; continue
    Bc = B - a[:,None]*(a@B)  # mean subtraction? a is probabilities sum1, phibar=a@B gives weighted mean.
    grad = d @ Bc
    gscale=np.linalg.norm(grad)
    if gscale small: try random theta perturbation
    for factor in [1,0.5,2]:
       th_try=th - lr*factor*grad/gscale
       # maybe clip theta [-4,4]
       a_try=sequence_from_theta
       sc=score
       accept best
    if improved lr=min(1,l*1.05) else lr*=0.8
    beta_cur = start + progress*...
    if stagnant > threshold: th += rng.normal(0,0.3,dim) (but keep th0 maybe)
```

4. `normalize_to_sum(a, target=1.0)`: ensures positive? We can allow zeros. For search, add floor.
```
def normalize_to_sum(a, target=1.0, floor=0.0):
  a=np.array(a,float)
  a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
  if target<=0: return a
  if floor>0: a=np.maximum(a,floor)
  for _ in range(5):
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0:
      a=np.full_like(a,target/a.size); break
    a *= target/s
    a=np.maximum(a, floor if floor>0 else 0)
  return a
```
If floor too high relative sum can fail; use small.
For search floor maybe 1e-12 but with sum1, floor*n =1e-8 for n=8192, fine.

```
For candidate score, reuse N from context.

```

10. `polish(a, budget, seed, nfft=None, base_beta=20, label='')`:

Implementation:
```
def polish(a, budget_s, seed, base_beta=20.0, label=''):
  a=normalize_to_sum(a,1.0,floor=1e-12)
  n=a.size; t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget
  if nfft None: Nconv=nextpow2(2*n-1); Nweighted=nextpow2(3*n-2)
  best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score_current(a)
  cur=a.copy(); score=best_score
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  mom=np.zeros(n)
  eta=0.15
  beta=base_beta
  noise=0.03
  accept_rel=0.0
  stagnant=0; bad=0; it=0
  progress=0
  while time<deadline:
    progress=(time-t0)/budget
    # schedule beta
    beta_cur = 8 + 80*progress + 10*noise? Actually beta higher later. Maybe base_beta + 100*progress? Use if base_beta.
    if not finite cur... reset
    c=fast_convolution(cur,Nconv)
    M=c.max()
    score=2*n*M
    if score<best_score: best=cur.copy(); best_score=score; stagnant=0
    if not finite M or M<=0: break
    w=make_weights(c, beta_cur)
    d=direction_from_weights(cur,w,Nweighted)
    if not finite or max(abs(d))<1e-14:
       # try antisymperturb
    else:
       mom=0.5*mom +0.5*d
       stepvec = eta*mom
       if noise>0: stepvec += noise*lowfreq_noise? maybe use full normal.
       stepvec=np.clip(stepvec,-2,2)
       # line search factors
       candidates=[]
       for f in (1.0,0.5,2.0):
          cand = cur * np.exp(-f*stepvec)
          cand=normalize_to_sum(cand,1.0,1e-12)
          candidates.append((f,cand))
       # evaluate each, choose best score; maybe also cur
       best_candidate=None; best_candidate_score=score
       for f,cand in candidates:
           c2=fast_convolution(cand,Nconv); sc2=2*n*c2.max()
           if finite and sc2<best_candidate_score:
               best_candidate=cand; best_candidate_score=sc2
       if best_candidate is not None:
           old=score; cur=best_candidate; score=best_candidate_score
           if score < old*(1-1e-10): eta=min(1.0, eta*1.08); stagnant=0; noise=max(0.002,noise*0.998)
           else: ...
           if score > old*(1+0.02): # accepted worsened? we only accept improvement over score; no annealing. Maybe add chance accept worse.
       else:
           eta*=0.7; mom*=0.5; stagnant++
    if stagnant>500 or bad>?: restart
    # occasional random perturb if stagnant >100
  return best,best_score
```
Need line search factors. `np.exp(-f*stepvec)` could underflow; clip stepvec to [-2,2] okay. Normalize.

if best_candidate_score <= score*(1+accept_prob?)...
```

if best_candidate_score < score: accept true
else:

   temp=max(1e-12, score*0.002*(1-progress)+1e-6/?)
   if rng.random() < np.exp(-delta/temp): accept
```
But accepting worsen may hurt best (still track). Good.

def random_smooth_log(n, rng, strength, antisym=False):

def sanitize(seq):
  seq=np.asarray(seq,float)
  if seq.size==0: return [1.0]*1024
  seq=np.where(np.isfinite(seq),seq,0); seq=np.clip(seq,0,1000)
  # scale to mean 1 if possible
  s=seq.sum(); n=seq.size
  if s>0 and seq.max()*n/s <=1000:
     seq=seq*n/s
  # if sum<0.01, fallback
  if s<0.01: return [1.0]*1024
  return [float(x) for x in seq]
```

`next_pow2(x)`:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    x=int(max(1,x))
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
```

`fast_convolution(a, nfft=None)`:
```
def fast_convolution(a, nfft=None):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    L=2*n-1
    if nfft is None:
        nfft=next_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, nfft)[:L]
    c=np.ascontiguousarray(c)
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c
```
If nfft too small for 2n-1? Ensure `nfft >= L` maybe. Caller. In score, N=next_pow2(2n) maybe (L). next_pow2(2n-1) gives power >= L? For n=2, L=3, next_pow2(3)=4. okay.

`fast_score_normalized(a)`:
```
def fast_score_normalized(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-30: return inf
    a=a/s
    c=fast_convolution(a)
    return float(2.0*n*c.max())
```
For repeated polish with nfft, maybe `score_with_nfft(a,nfft)`.

`fast_score_norm_nfft(a, nfft)`:
```
c=fast_convolution(a,nfft); return 2*n*c.max()
```
Assume normalized.

`normalize_to_sum(a, target=1.0, floor=0.0)`:
```
def normalize_to_sum(a, target=1.0, floor=0.0):
    a=np.array(a,dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if a.size==0: return a
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if target <=0 or a.size==0:
        return a
    if floor >0:
        a=np.maximum(a, floor)
    for _ in range(6):
        s=float(a.sum())
        if s<=1e-300:
            a.fill(target/max(1,a.size))
            break
        a *= target/s
        if floor >0:
            a=np.maximum(a, floor)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=1e-300:
        a.fill(target/max(1,a.size))
    else:
        a *= target/s
        if floor>0: a=np.maximum(a,floor)
    return a
```
If floor too big, repeated scaling could exceed target. floor=1e-12, n=8192, floor sum 8e-9 negligible. okay.

`weighted_convolution(a,w,nfft)`:
```
def weighted_convolution(a,w,nfft=None):
  n=a.size
  L=2*n-1
  if nfft is None:
     nfft=next_pow2(3*n-2)
  wr=np.ascontiguousarray(w[::-1])
  A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
  W=np.fft.rfft(wr,nfft)
  out=np.fft.irfft(A*W,nfft)
  return out[L-1:L-1+n].copy()
```
Need nfft >= n + (2n-1) -1 =3n-2. next_pow2(3*n-2). For n=1? L=1, nfft next_pow2(1)=1, slice [0:1] ok. If n large, okay.

`make_weights(c, beta, mix=0.03)`:
```
def make_weights(c, beta, mix=0.03):
    L=c.size
    M=float(c.max())
    if L==0 or M<=0:
        return np.full(L,1.0/L if L>0 else 0)
    x=beta*(c/M -1.0)
    x=np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0)
    w=np.exp(x)
    sw=float(w.sum())
    if sw<=0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    else:
        w/=sw
    if mix>0:
        w = (1.0-mix)*w + (mix/L)
        sw=w.sum(); if sw>0: w/=sw
    return w
```

`direction_from_a_weights(a, w, nfft)`:
```
def direction_from_a_weights(a,w,nfft):
  n=a.size
  W=weighted_convolution(a,w,nfft)
  G=a*W
  aa=float(a@a)
  if aa<=0: return np.zeros(n)
  mu=float(a@G)/aa
  d=G - mu*a
  # scale
  ad2=float(a@(d*d))
  scale=np.sqrt(ad2) if ad2>0 else 0.0
  if scale<1e-15:
     scale=np.max(np.abs(d))
  if scale<1e-15:
     return np.zeros(n)
  return d/scale
```

`lowfreq_noise(n, rng, strength, antisym=False)`:
```
def lowfreq_noise(n, rng, strength, antisym=False):
   z=np.zeros(n)
   if n<2: return z
   t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   if antisym:
      x=2*t-1
      for m in range(1,9):
         amp=strength/(0.7+0.3*m)
         z += amp*rng.normal(0.0,1.0)*np.sin(np.pi*m*x)
   else:
      for m in range(1,8):
         amp=strength/(0.7+0.3*m)
         z += amp*(rng.normal(0,1)*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t + rng.uniform(0,2π)) + rng.normal(0,1)*np.sin(...))
   return z
```
Ensure rng.normal scalar.

`sequence_from_theta(B, theta)`:
```
def sequence_from_theta(B, theta):
  z=B.dot(theta)
  zmax=float(z.max())
  a=np.exp(z-zmax)
  s=float(a.sum())
  if s<=0 or not finite:
     a=np.ones(B.shape[0])
  else: a/=s
  return np.maximum(a,1e-300)
```

`make_trig_basis(n, dim)`:
```
def make_trig_basis(n, dim):
   B=np.zeros((n,dim), dtype=np.float64)
   if dim<=0: return B
   x=np.linspace(-1.0,1.0,n)
   col=0
   m=1
   while col<dim and m<=32:
      # odd sine
      if col<dim:
         B[:,col]=np.sin(np.pi*m*x); col+=1
      # even cosine
      if col<dim:
         B[:,col]=np.cos(np.pi*m*x); col+=1
      m+=1
   return B
```
This gives alternating sine/cos. Sine modes all odd around center? sin(mπ(-x))=-sin(mπx): yes. Cos even. Good.
Maybe include low-frequency in t not x? This is okay.

`project_sequence_to_basis(a, B)`:
```
def project_sequence_to_basis(a,B):
   n=a.size; dim=B.shape[1]
   loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15))
   loga -= float(loga.mean())
   if dim==0: return np.zeros(0)
   try:
      theta, res, rank, s = np.linalg.lstsq(B, loga, rcond=None)
      return theta
   except: return np.zeros(dim)
```

def basis_search(n, budget_s, seed, dim, beta_start=8.0, beta_end=100.0, start_seq=None):
   if budget_s <=1: return None,inf,start_seq
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s

   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

      a0=normalize_to_sum(start_seq,1.0,1e-12)

Let's code `polish` carefully:
```
def polish(a, budget_s, seed, min_budget=1.0, label=''):
  if not finite budget return a,inf
  t0=...
  a=normalize_to_sum(a,1.0,1e-12)
  n=a.size
  Nconv=next_pow2(2*n-1); Nw=next_pow2(3*n-2)
  c=fast_convolution(a,Nconv); score=2*n*c.max()
  best=a.copy(); best_score=score
  cur=a.copy()
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  mom=np.zeros(n)
  eta=0.25
  noise=0.03
  last_best=t0
  it=0
  while time<deadline:
    it+=1
    if not finite or sum<=0: cur=best.copy(); score=best_score; mom=0
    c=fast_convolution(cur,Nconv); M=c.max(); score=2*n*M
    if score<best_score: best=cur.copy(); best_score=score; last_best=time.monotonic(); stag=0
    # schedule progress
    progress=(time-t0)/budget
    beta=8+120*progress
    w=make_weights(c,beta)
    d=direction_from_a_weights(cur,w,Nw)
    if maxabs(d)>1e-14:
       mom=0.5*mom+0.5*d
       mom_clip=np.clip(mom,-3,3)
       step=mom_clip
       if noise>1e-5:
          step = step + noise*lowfreq_noise(n,rng,1.0, antisym=(it%3==0))
       step=np.clip(step,-1.2,1.2)
       best_trial=None; best_trial_score=score
       temp=max(1e-9, best_score*0.001*(1.0-progress))
       for f in (eta,0.5*eta,2.0*eta):
          trial=log_update(cur, f*step, 1e-12)
          c2=fast_convolution(trial,Nconv); sc2=2*n*c2.max()
          if finite and (sc2 < best_trial_score or (sc2 < score + temp and rng.random()<np.exp(-(sc2-score)/temp))):
             best_trial=trial; best_trial_score=sc2
       if best_trial is not None:
          old=score; cur=best_trial; score=best_trial_score
          if score < old*(1-1e-10): eta=min(1.2, eta*1.05); noise=max(0.005,noise*0.999); mom*=0.9
          else: eta=max(0.03, eta*0.8)
       else:
          eta=max(0.02,eta*0.7); mom*=0.5
    else:
       eta=...; mom*=0.5
    if time-last_best > max(10.0, 0.04*budget_s) and it>20:
       # restart
       strength=0.3 + 0.4*min(1.0, it/5000)
       z=lowfreq_noise(n,rng,strength, antisym=True)
       cand=normalize_to_sum(cur*(1+? exp) ...)
       # try several strengths / starts from best
       improved=False
       for st in (0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0):
           zz=lowfreq_noise(n,rng,st, antisym=(st>0.3))
           cc=normalize_to_sum(best*np.exp(np.clip(zz,-1.5,1.5)),1.0,1e-12)
           csc=fast_convolution(cc,Nconv).max()
           scsc=2*n*csc
           if scsc < best_score*1.02:
              cur=cc; score=scsc; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           zz=...; cur=...; score=...
       mom*=0.2; eta=0.25; noise=0.05; last_best=time.monotonic() if score<best_score? don't fake.
  return best,best_score
```

Time management in propose:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=float(budget_s)
    if total < 5: return sanitize(np.ones(1024))
    reserve = min(60.0, 0.03*total)
    hard_deadline = t0 + total - reserve
    # choose sizes
    if total < 300:
       n_explore=512; n_main=1024; n_final=1024; explore_budget=0
    elif total < 900:
       n_explore=1024; n_main=2048; n_final=4096; explore_budget=np.min(120, 0.15*total)
    elif total < 1800:
       n_explore=2048; n_main=4096; n_final=6733; explore_budget=np.min(240, 0.15*total)
    else:
       n_explore=2048; n_main=4096; n_final=10000; explore_budget=min(420, 0.18*total)
    parent = get_parent()
    pool=[]
    # quick starts at n_main
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    starts=generate_starts(n_main,rng,parent,count=20)
    for s in starts: pool_add(pool,s,0)
    # explore basis at n_explore if time
    explore_deadline=t0+explore_budget
    if explore_deadline>t0+10:
       dims=(12, 24, 36)
       per=(explore_budget-t0)/len(dims)
       for i,dim in enumerate(dims):
           if per < 5: break
           seq,sc=basis_search(n_explore, min(per, explore_deadline-time), seed+1000+i, dim, start_seq=parent if parent else None)
           if seq is not None:
               pool_add(pool, upsample_sequence(seq,n_main),0)
       # also add upsample parent to n_main if not
    # Polish n_main
    main_reserve = 0.0
    if n_final > n_main and total > 900:
        main_reserve = min(0.45*total, 900.0)
    main_deadline = t0 + max(60, total - reserve - main_reserve)

        seq=pool[0][1]

        if alloc<10: break
        newseq,sc=polish(seq, alloc, seed+9000+iters)
        iters+=1
        pool_add(pool,newseq,0)
    # final
    pool.sort; best=pool[0][1]
    if n_final>best.size:
       best=upsample_sequence(best,n_final); pool_add(pool,best,0)
    else:
       pool_add(pool,best,0)
    final_deadline=hard_deadline
    iters=0

       remain=final_deadline-time

       pool_add
       iters+=1
    # if time left and final score > target maybe polish more
```

Implementation:
```
def sanitize_for_return(seq):
    seq=np.asarray(seq,float)
    if seq.size==0: return [1.0]*1024
    seq=np.where(np.isfinite(seq),seq,0.0)
    seq=np.clip(seq,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(seq.sum())
    n=seq.size
    if s < 0.01:
       return [1.0]*1024
    scale = n / s
    if float(seq.max())*scale <= 1000.0:
       seq = seq * scale
       seq = np.clip(seq,0.0,1000.0)
       if seq.sum() < 0.01: return [1.0]*n
    else:
       # reduce max clipping while preserving sum maybe
       seq=np.clip(seq,0,1000)
    return [float(x) for x in seq]
```
If scaling causes sum not exactly n due clip, okay.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The code uses FFT-based objective evaluation, a low-dimensional smooth
basis optimizer to escape the symmetric uniform saddle, and a full-variable
log-space smoothed-max descent with annealing, momentum, and occasional
antisymmetric restarts.  The best candidates are upscaled to a final length
and polished.  A few final candidates are validated with the exact
`evaluate_sequence` function before returning the best valid list.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Small utilities
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def pair_score(pair):
    return pair[0]


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_to_sum(a, target=1.0, floor=0.0):
    """Return a non-negative array scaled to the requested sum."""
    a = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    if target <= 0.0:
        return a

    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, floor)

    for _ in range(4):
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            a.fill(target / float(max(1, a.size)))
            break
        a *= target / s

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a.fill(target / float(max(1, a.size)))
    else:
        a *= target / s
    return a


def sanitize_for_return(seq):
    """Convert an array to a valid Python list of non-negative finite floats."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return [1.0] * 1024

    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(seq.sum())
    n = int(seq.size)
    if s < 0.01:
        return [1.0] * max(1024, n)

    scale = float(n) / s
    if float(seq.max()) * scale <= 1000.0:
        seq = seq * scale
        seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(seq.sum()) < 0.01:
            return [1.0] * max(1024, n)

    return [float(x) for x in seq]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# FFT-based objective and derivatives
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def fast_convolution(a, nfft=None):
    """Linear convolution a * a using FFT; returns length 2n-1."""
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    if nfft is None:
        nfft = next_pow2(L)
    elif nfft < L:
        nfft = next_pow2(L)

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, nfft)[:L]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def fast_score_general(a):
    """Fast analogue of evaluate_sequence for an arbitrary array."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")

    c = fast_convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * float(c.max()) / (s * s))


def weighted_convolution(a, w, nfft=None):
    """
    For weights w_k on convolution coefficients c_k, compute
        W_j = sum_k w_k * a_{k-j}
    for j = 0,...,n-1.
    """
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if nfft is None:
        nfft = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    elif nfft < (3 * n - 2):
        nfft = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    wr = np.ascontiguousarray(w[::-1], dtype=np.float64)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    W = np.fft.rfft(wr, nfft)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * W, nfft)

    # The desired indices are p = L-1-j, i.e. descending from L-1 to n-1.
    return np.ascontiguousarray(out[n - 1:L][::-1], dtype=np.float64)


def make_weights(c, beta, mix=0.04):
    """Soft-max weights on convolution coefficients, with a small uniform mix."""
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)

    x = beta * (c / M - 1.0)
    x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(x)
    sw = float(w.sum())

    if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= sw

    if mix > 0.0:
        w = (1.0 - mix) * w + (mix / L)
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw > 0.0:
            w /= sw

    return w


def direction_from_a_weights(a, w, nfft):
    """
    Normalized tangent direction in log-space for the weighted objective
        sum_k w_k * c_k(a),
    projected to preserve the L1 scale.
    """
    n = a.size
    W = weighted_convolution(a, w, nfft)
    G = a * W

    aa = float(a @ a)
    if aa <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(aa):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    mu = float(a @ G) / aa
    d = G - mu * a

    scale_sq = float(a @ (d * d))
    if not np.isfinite(scale_sq) or scale_sq <= 0.0:
        scale_sq = float(np.max(np.abs(d))) * float(np.max(np.abs(d)))

    scale = math.sqrt(scale_sq) if scale_sq > 0.0 else 0.0
    if scale <= 1e-15:
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if scale <= 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    d = d / scale
    return np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)


def log_update(a, step, floor=1e-15):
    """Multiplicative log update followed by normalization."""
    step = np.clip(step, -3.0, 3.0)
    b = a * np.exp(step)
    return normalize_to_sum(b, 1.0, floor)


def lowfreq_noise(n, rng, strength, antisym=False):
    """Smooth random log-noise; antisymmetric variants help leave symmetric saddles."""
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 2 or strength <= 0.0:
        return z

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = 2.0 * t - 1.0
    two_pi = 2.0 * np.pi

    if antisym:
        # sin(m*pi*x) is odd about x=0, hence antisymmetric under reversal.
        for m in range(1, 9):
            amp = strength / (0.7 + 0.35 * m)
            z += amp * float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * np.sin(math.pi * m * x)
    else:
        z += strength * float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * x
        for m in range(1, 8):
            amp = strength / (0.7 + 0.35 * m)
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, two_pi))
            z += amp * (
                float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * np.cos(two_pi * m * t + ph) +
                float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * np.sin(two_pi * m * t + ph)
            )

    return z


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Candidate constructions
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def upsample_sequence(seq, n):
    """Linearly interpolate a sequence to length n and normalize to sum 1."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return np.full(int(n), 1.0 / max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)

    m = seq.size
    n = int(n)
    if m == n:
        return normalize_to_sum(seq, 1.0, 1e-15)

    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(xnew, xold, np.maximum(seq, 0.0))
    return normalize_to_sum(out, 1.0, 1e-15)


def make_two_block(n, frac, ratio):
    """Simple two-level block sequence."""
    n = int(n)
    m = int(round(float(frac) * n))
    if m < 1:
        m = 1
    if m >= n:
        m = n - 1

    denom = float(m) * float(ratio) + float(n - m)
    if denom <= 0.0:
        B = 1.0
    else:
        B = float(n) / denom
    A = float(ratio) * B

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = A
    a[m:] = B
    return normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0, 1e-15)


def generate_starts(n, rng, parent=None, count=20):
    """Generate a pool of diverse normalized starts."""
    n = int(n)
    starts = []
    if n <= 0:
        return starts

    # Uniform baseline.
    starts.append(np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64))

    # Parent / previous best, if available.
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        starts.append(upsample_sequence(parent, n))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = 2.0 * t - 1.0

    # Two-block starts.
    for frac in (0.12, 0.25, 0.35, 0.45, 0.55, 0.70):
        for ratio in (0.25, 0.6, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0):
            starts.append(make_two_block(n, frac, ratio))

    # Deterministic asymmetric smooth shapes.
    for alpha in (0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.3, 1.8):
        for beta in (0.0, -0.35, 0.0, 0.35, 0.6):
            f = 1.0 + alpha * np.sin(math.pi * x) + beta * np.sin(3.0 * math.pi * x)
            f = np.maximum(f, 1e-8)
            starts.append(normalize_to_sum(f, 1.0, 1e-15))

            f = 1.0 - alpha * np.sin(math.pi * x) - beta * np.sin(3.0 * math.pi * x)
            f = np.maximum(f, 1e-8)
            starts.append(normalize_to_sum(f, 1.0, 1e-15))

    for zlo, zhi in ((-1.0, -0.2), (-1.0, 0.2), (-1.0, 1.0), (1.0, 1.0)):
        z = zlo * x + 0.3 * zhi * np.cos(math.pi * x)
        starts.append(normalize_to_sum(np.exp(np.clip(z, -5.0, 5.0)), 1.0, 1e-15))

    # Random smooth starts.
    for _ in range(max(0, int(count))):
        z = 0.9 * float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * x
        for m in range(1, 10):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)) / (m ** 0.55)
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            z += amp * (
                float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph) +
                float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph)
            )
        a = np.exp(np.clip(z, -5.0, 5.0))
        if rng.random() < 0.2:
            # Occasionally insert a smooth block-like valley/peak.
            w = max(1, int(n * float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))))
            s0 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
            s1 = min(n, s0 + w)
            z_block = -1.5 * float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0)) * np.cos(
                math.pi * (np.arange(s1 - s0) / max(1, (s1 - s0 - 1)))
            )
            z[s0:s1] += z_block
            a = np.exp(np.clip(z, -5.0, 5.0))
        starts.append(normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0, 1e-15))

    return starts


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Candidate pool
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def pool_add(pool, seq, max_keep=25):
    """Add a sequence with recomputed fast score, sorting and trimming pool."""
    if seq is None:
        return

    try:
        a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        a = normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0, 1e-15)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            return

        c = fast_convolution(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            return
        sc = float(2.0 * a.size * float(c.max()) / (s * s))
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            return

        pool.append([sc, a])
        pool.sort(key=pair_score)
        if len(pool) > max_keep:
            pool.pop()
    except Exception:
        return


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Low-dimensional basis optimizer
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_trig_basis(n, dim):
    """
    Trigonometric basis centered at x=2*t-1.
    Even-indexed columns are odd sine modes, useful for breaking symmetry.
    """
    n = int(n)
    dim = int(dim)
    B = np.zeros((n, dim), dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0 or dim == 0:
        return B

    x = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    col = 0
    m = 1
    while col < dim and m <= 40:
        if col < dim:
            B[:, col] = np.sin(math.pi * m * x)
            col += 1
        if col < dim:
            B[:, col] = np.cos(math.pi * m * x)
            col += 1
        m += 1
    return B


def sequence_from_theta(B, theta):
    """Convert basis coefficients to a positive normalized sequence."""
    z = B.dot(theta)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    a = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(B.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = a / s
    return np.maximum(a, 1e-300)


def project_sequence_to_basis(a, B):
    """Least-squares projection of log(a) onto basis B."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    dim = B.shape[1]
    if dim == 0 or a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)

    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-15))
    loga -= float(loga.mean())

    try:
        theta, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(B, loga, rcond=None)
        theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
        return np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
    except Exception:
        return np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)


def basis_search(n, budget_s, seed, dim, beta_start=8.0, beta_end=110.0, start_seq=None):
    """
    Optimize a low-dimensional log-basis parameterization using the same
    smoothed-max log-gradient as the full search.
    """
    if budget_s <= 1.0:
        a0 = start_seq
        if a0 is None:
            a0 = np.full(int(n), 1.0 / max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
        return a0, fast_score_general(a0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    n = int(n)
    dim = int(max(1, dim))
    B = make_trig_basis(n, dim)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    Nscore = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Nweight = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    if start_seq is not None and np.asarray(start_seq).size > 0:
        a0 = upsample_sequence(start_seq, n)
        theta = project_sequence_to_basis(a0, B)
        theta += rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, dim)
    else:
        theta = rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, dim)
        if dim > 0:
            sign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
            theta[0] = sign * float(rng.uniform(0.9, 1.8))

    theta = np.clip(theta, -5.0, 5.0)
    a = sequence_from_theta(B, theta)

    c = fast_convolution(a, Nscore)
    score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    eta = 0.35
    beta = float(beta_start)
    stagnant = 0
    last_best_time = t0
    last_restart_time = t0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        now = time.monotonic()
        progress = (now - t0) / max(1e-12, (deadline - t0))

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
            a = best.copy()
            score = best_score
            theta = np.clip(theta, -5.0, 5.0)

        c = fast_convolution(a, Nscore)
        M = float(c.max())
        score = float(2.0 * n * M)

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = score
            last_best_time = now
            stagnant = 0

        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        beta = beta_start + (beta_end - beta_start) * progress
        w = make_weights(c, beta, mix=0.05)
        d = direction_from_a_weights(a, w, Nweight)

        grad = d @ B
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))

        if gnorm > 1e-14:
            best_trial = a.copy()
            best_trial_score = score
            best_theta = theta.copy()
            moved = False
            temp = min(score * 0.01, max(1e-10, best_score * 0.002 * max(0.0, 1.0 - progress)))

            for factor in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 2.0 * eta):
                th_try = theta - factor * (grad / gnorm)
                th_try = np.clip(th_try, -5.0, 5.0)
                a_try = sequence_from_theta(B, th_try)
                c_try = fast_convolution(a_try, Nscore)
                sc_try = float(2.0 * n * c_try.max())

                if not np.isfinite(sc_try):
                    continue

                accept = False
                if sc_try < best_trial_score - 1e-14:
                    accept = True
                elif sc_try <= score + temp and temp > 0.0:
                    prob = math.exp(-max(0.0, sc_try - score) / temp)
                    if rng.random() < prob:
                        accept = True

                if accept:
                    best_trial = a_try
                    best_trial_score = sc_try
                    best_theta = th_try
                    moved = True

            if moved:
                old = score
                theta = best_theta
                a = best_trial
                score = best_trial_score
                if score < old * (1.0 - 1e-9):
                    eta = min(1.2, eta * 1.09)
                    stagnant = 0
                else:
                    eta = max(0.07, eta * 0.85)
            else:
                eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.72)
                stagnant += 1
        else:
            theta += rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, dim)
            theta = np.clip(theta, -5.0, 5.0)
            a = sequence_from_theta(B, theta)
            c = fast_convolution(a, Nscore)
            score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
            eta = max(0.08, eta * 0.85)
            stagnant += 1

        if now - last_best_time > max(8.0, 0.06 * (deadline - t0)) and it > 20:
            if now - last_restart_time > max(6.0, 0.03 * (deadline - t0)):
                last_restart_time = now
                theta += rng.normal(0.0, 0.55, dim)
                theta = np.clip(theta, -5.0, 5.0)
                a = sequence_from_theta(B, theta)
                c = fast_convolution(a, Nscore)
                score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
                eta = 0.35
                stagnant = 0

    c_best = fast_convolution(best, Nscore)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * c_best.max()) if c_best.size else fast_score_general(best)
    return best, best_score


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Full-variable polish
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def polish(a_init, budget_s, seed, label="polish"):
    """Full log-space smoothed-max local search."""
    if budget_s <= 0.5 or a_init is None:
        seq = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64) if a_init is not None else np.ones(1024)
        return seq, fast_score_general(seq)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    a = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        n = 1024

    a = normalize_to_sum(a, 1.0, 1e-15)

    Nscore = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Nweight = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    cur = a.copy()
    c = fast_convolution(cur, Nscore)
    s = float(cur.sum())
    score = float(2.0 * n * c.max() / (s * s))
    best = cur.copy()
    best_score = score

    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    eta = 0.28
    noise = 0.025
    last_best_time = t0
    last_restart_time = t0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        now = time.monotonic()
        progress = (now - t0) / max(1e-12, (deadline - t0))

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(cur)) or float(cur.sum()) <= 0.0:
            cur = best.copy()
            c = fast_convolution(cur, Nscore)
            s = float(cur.sum())
            score = float(2.0 * n * c.max() / (s * s))
            mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        c = fast_convolution(cur, Nscore)
        s = float(cur.sum())
        M = float(c.max())
        score = float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s))

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best = cur.copy()
            best_score = score
            last_best_time = now

        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(score):
            break

        # If current has drifted far above best, return to best.
        if score > best_score * 1.08:
            cur = best.copy()
            c = fast_convolution(cur, Nscore)
            s = float(cur.sum())
            score = float(2.0 * n * c.max() / (s * s))
            mom *= 0.2
            eta = 0.25
            noise = 0.04

        beta = 8.0 + 130.0 * progress
        w = make_weights(c, beta, mix=0.06)
        d = direction_from_a_weights(cur, w, Nweight)

        if float(np.max(np.abs(d))) > 1e-14:
            mom = 0.5 * mom + 0.5 * d
            mom = np.clip(mom, -3.0, 3.0)
            step = mom.copy()
            if noise > 1e-6:
                step = step + noise * lowfreq_noise(n, rng, 1.0, antisym=(it % 3 == 0))
            step = np.clip(step, -1.5, 1.5)

            best_trial = None
            best_trial_score = score
            moved = False
            temp = min(score * 0.008, max(1e-10, best_score * 0.0012 * max(0.0, 1.0 - progress)))

            for factor in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 2.0 * eta):
                trial = log_update(cur, factor * step, 1e-15)
                c2 = fast_convolution(trial, Nscore)
                s2 = float(trial.sum())
                sc2 = float(2.0 * n * c2.max() / (s2 * s2))

                if not np.isfinite(sc2):
                    continue

                accept = False
                if sc2 < best_trial_score - 1e-14:
                    accept = True
                elif sc2 <= score + temp and temp > 0.0:
                    prob = math.exp(-max(0.0, sc2 - score) / temp)
                    if rng.random() < prob:
                        accept = True

                if accept:
                    best_trial = trial
                    best_trial_score = sc2
                    moved = True

            if moved and best_trial is not None:
                old = score
                cur = best_trial
                score = best_trial_score
                if score < old * (1.0 - 1e-9):
                    eta = min(1.0, eta * 1.06)
                    noise = max(0.004, noise * 0.9985)
                    mom *= 0.88
                else:
                    eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.86)
            else:
                eta = max(0.04, eta * 0.72)
                mom *= 0.5
        else:
            eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.85)
            mom *= 0.5

        # Antisymmetric restarts from best when no meaningful improvement occurs.
        if now - last_best_time > max(10.0, 0.035 * (deadline - t0)) and it > 25:
            if now - last_restart_time > max(8.0, 0.025 * (deadline - t0)):
                last_restart_time = now
                replaced = False
                for st in (0.08, 0.18, 0.35, 0.65, 1.0):
                    z = lowfreq_noise(n, rng, st, antisym=True)
                    cand = normalize_to_sum(best * np.exp(np.clip(z, -2.5, 2.5)), 1.0, 1e-15)
                    cc = fast_convolution(cand, Nscore)
                    sc = float(2.0 * n * cc.max())
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best = cand.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                    if sc < score * 1.02 or sc < best_score:
                        cur = cand
                        score = sc
                        replaced = True
                        break

                if not replaced:
                    z = lowfreq_noise(n, rng, 0.35, antisym=True)
                    cur = normalize_to_sum(best * np.exp(np.clip(z, -2.0, 2.0)), 1.0, 1e-15)
                    cc = fast_convolution(cur, Nscore)
                    score = float(2.0 * n * cc.max())

                mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                eta = 0.28
                noise = 0.045

    c_best = fast_convolution(best, Nscore)
    s_best = float(best.sum())
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * c_best.max() / (s_best * s_best)) if c_best.size else fast_score_general(best)
    return best, best_score


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Parent access
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if obj is None:
            return None
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return None
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.sum() <= 0.0:
            return None
        return normalize_to_sum(arr, 1.0, 0.0)
    except Exception:
        return None


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main search entry point
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.

    The function tries to improve a smooth asymmetric shape at increasing
    sequence lengths, then polishes the best final-length candidate.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve_exact = min(75.0, max(30.0, 0.04 * total))
    hard_deadline = t0 + total - reserve_exact

    # Choose sizes based on available time.
    if total < 300.0:
        n_explore = 512
        n_main = 1024
        n_final = 1024
        explore_budget = 0.0
    elif total < 900.0:
        n_explore = 1024
        n_main = 2048
        n_final = 4096
        explore_budget = min(150.0, 0.18 * total)
    elif total < 1800.0:
        n_explore = 2048
        n_main = 4096
        n_final = 6733
        explore_budget = min(270.0, 0.18 * total)
    else:
        n_explore = 2048
        n_main = 4096
        n_final = 8192
        explore_budget = min(430.0, 0.18 * total)

    if explore_budget > 0.5 * total:
        explore_budget = 0.45 * total

    final_reserve = 0.0
    if n_final > n_main and total > 500.0:
        final_reserve = min(0.45 * total, max(180.0, 0.38 * total))
        final_reserve = min(final_reserve, 0.65 * total)

    main_available = total - reserve_exact - explore_budget - final_reserve
    if main_available < 120.0:
        main_available = max(60.0, total - reserve_exact - 60.0 - explore_budget)
        final_reserve = max(0.0, total - reserve_exact - explore_budget - main_available)

    main_deadline = t0 + max(30.0, main_available)
    if main_deadline > hard_deadline:
        main_deadline = hard_deadline

    print(f"[start] budget={total:.1f}s explore={int(n_explore)} main={int(n_main)} final={int(n_final)}", flush=True)

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    seed_base = int(seed)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Build a diverse main-length pool.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    main_pool = []
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_base)

    starts = generate_starts(n_main, rng, parent, count=24)
    for seq in starts:
        pool_add(main_pool, seq, max_keep=35)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Low-dimensional exploration at a smaller or medium length.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    if explore_budget > 20.0:
        explore_deadline = t0 + explore_budget
        dims = (12, 24, 36, 48)
        # Use only as many basis runs as time remains.
        dur = max(0.0, explore_deadline - time.monotonic())
        active_dims = []
        for dim in dims:
            if dur > 25.0:
                active_dims.append(dim)
                dur /= 1.7
        if not active_dims:
            active_dims = [12]

        for idx, dim in enumerate(active_dims):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now + 5.0 > explore_deadline:
                break
            alloc = min((explore_deadline - now) * 0.92, 300.0)
            try:
                seq, sc = basis_search(
                    n_explore,
                    alloc,
                    seed=seed_base + 1000 + idx * 17,
                    dim=dim,
                    start_seq=parent,
                    beta_start=8.0,
                    beta_end=120.0
                )
                if seq is not None:
                    upseq = upsample_sequence(seq, n_main)
                    pool_add(main_pool, upseq, max_keep=35)
                    print(f"[basis] dim={dim} score={sc:.9f}", flush=True)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[basis] dim={dim} failed: {e}", flush=True)

    # Ensure parent is represented.
    if parent is not None:
        pool_add(main_pool, upsample_sequence(parent, n_main), max_keep=35)

    main_pool.sort(key=pair_score)
    if main_pool:
        print(f"[main pool] top={main_pool[0][0]:.9f}", flush=True)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main-length polish on top candidates.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    main_passes = 4 if total > 1500.0 else (3 if total > 700.0 else 2)
    last_top_score = None

    for pass_idx in range(main_passes):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 30.0 >= main_deadline:
            break

        remain = main_deadline - now
        alloc = min(remain * (0.48 if pass_idx == 0 else 0.42), 1100.0)
        if alloc < 25.0:
            break

        main_pool.sort(key=pair_score)
        if not main_pool:
            break

        idx = 0
        if len(main_pool) > 1 and last_top_score is not None and abs(main_pool[0][0] - last_top_score) < 1e-12:
            idx = min(1, len(main_pool) - 1)

        seq = main_pool[idx][1]
        before = main_pool[idx][0]

        try:
            newseq, sc = polish(seq, alloc, seed=seed_base + 9000 + pass_idx * 31, label="main")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[polish main {pass_idx}] failed: {e}", flush=True)
            newseq, sc = seq, before

        pool_add(main_pool, newseq, max_keep=40)
        main_pool.sort(key=pair_score)
        last_top_score = main_pool[0][0]
        print(f"[main polish {pass_idx}] before={before:.9f} after_best={last_top_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    main_pool.sort(key=pair_score)
    if main_pool:
        print(f"[main best] fast={main_pool[0][0]:.9f} len={main_pool[0][1].size}", flush=True)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final-length polish.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    final_pool = []

    if n_final > 0 and main_pool:
        top_main = main_pool[:min(3, len(main_pool))]
        for seq in top_main:
            final_pool.add(upsample_sequence(seq, n_final)) if False else pool_add(final_pool, upsample_sequence(seq, n_final), max_keep=12)
    else:
        pool_add(final_pool, np.ones(n_final, dtype=np.float64) / n_final, max_keep=12)

    final_passes = 3 if total > 1500.0 else (2 if total > 700.0 else 1)
    last_final_score = None

    for pass_idx in range(final_passes):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 30.0 >= hard_deadline:
            break

        remain = hard_deadline - now
        alloc = min(remain * (0.52 if pass_idx == 0 else 0.42), 1500.0)
        if alloc < 25.0:
            break

        final_pool.sort(key=pair_score)
        if not final_pool:
            break

        idx = 0
        if len(final_pool) > 1 and last_final_score is not None and abs(final_pool[0][0] - last_final_score) < 1e-12:
            idx = min(1, len(final_pool) - 1)

        seq = final_pool[idx][1]
        before = final_pool[idx][0]

        try:
            newseq, sc = polish(seq, alloc, seed=seed_base + 19000 + pass_idx * 41, label="final")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[polish final {pass_idx}] failed: {e}", flush=True)
            newseq, sc = seq, before

        pool_add(final_pool, newseq, max_keep=12)
        final_pool.sort(key=pair_score)
        last_final_score = final_pool[0][0]
        print(f"[final polish {pass_idx}] before={before:.9f} after_best={last_final_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    # Prefer final-length candidates if available; otherwise use main.
    candidate_pool = final_pool if final_pool else main_pool
    if not candidate_pool:
        return sanitize_for_return(np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64) / n_main)

    candidate_pool.sort(key=pair_score)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Exact validation with evaluate_sequence, if available.
    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    exact_best_seq = candidate_pool[0][1]
    exact_best_score = candidate_pool[0][0] if np.isfinite(candidate_pool[0][0]) else float("inf")

    eval_func = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    max_exact = 3 if candidate_pool[0][1].size <= 4096 else 2

    for i in range(min(max_exact, len(candidate_pool))):
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline:
            break
        seq = candidate_pool[i][1]
        try:
            lst = sanitize_for_return(seq)
            if eval_func is not None:
                ex = float(eval_func(lst))
            else:
                ex = float(fast_score_general(seq))
            if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < exact_best_score:
                exact_best_score = ex
                exact_best_seq = seq
            print(f"[exact] candidate {i}: {ex:.10f}", flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[exact] failed candidate {i}: {e}", flush=True)
            continue

    if not np.isfinite(exact_best_score):
        exact_best_seq = candidate_pool[0][1]

    return sanitize_for_return(exact_best_seq)
```
